{"forms":[{"form":"medians","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A central vein or nerve, especially the median vein or median nerve running through the forearm and arm."],"tags":["archaic"],"topics":["anatomy","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A line segment joining the vertex of triangle to the midpoint of the opposing side."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A number separating the higher half from the lower half of a data sample, population, or probability distribution. The median of a finite list of numbers can be found by arranging all the observations from lowest value to highest value and picking the middle one (e.g., the median of {3, 3, 5, 9, 11} is 5). {"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"sheets","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Use the sheets in the hall closet to make the bed.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Bedsheet; a thin bed cloth used as a covering for a mattress or as a layer over the sleeper."]},{"examples":[{"text":"A sheet of paper measuring eight and one-half inches wide by eleven inches high is a popular item in commerce.","type":"example"},{"text":"Paper is designated “20 pound” if a stack (ream) of 500 sheets 22 inches by 17 inches weighs 20 pounds.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of paper, usually rectangular, that has been prepared for writing, artwork, drafting, wrapping, manufacture of packaging (boxes, envelopes, etc.), and for other uses. The word does not include scraps and irregular small pieces destined to be recycled, used for stuffing or cushioning or papier-mâché, etc. In modern books, each sheet of paper is typically folded in half, to produce two leaves and four pages. In the absence of folding, \"leaf\" and \"sheet\" are equivalent."]},{"examples":[{"text":"rap sheet, style sheet, material safety datasheet, balance sheet","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of paper, usually rectangular, that has been prepared for writing, artwork, drafting, wrapping, manufacture of packaging (boxes, envelopes, etc.), and for other uses. The word does not include scraps and irregular small pieces destined to be recycled, used for stuffing or cushioning or papier-mâché, etc. In modern books, each sheet of paper is typically folded in half, to produce two leaves and four pages. In the absence of folding, \"leaf\" and \"sheet\" are equivalent.","(originally referring to a printed document) A summary or record of key information, especially an official one; the information contained in such a document."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Place the rolls on the cookie sheet, edges touching, and bake for 10-11 minutes.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A flat metal pan, often without raised edge, used for baking."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The glazer cut several panes from a large sheet of glass.","type":"example"},{"text":"A sheet of that new silicon stuff is as good as a sheet of tinfoil to keep food from sticking in the baking pan.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A thin, flat piece or layer of solid material."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Mud froze on the road in a solid sheet, then more rain froze into a sheet of ice on top of the mud!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A broad, flat expanse or covering of a material on a surface."]},{"glosses":["(Usually used in the plural) An expanse or intense wave of something fluid (such as fire or precipitation), conceptualized as hanging, suspended, falling, or moving in a particular direction."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"To be \"three sheets to the wind\" is to say that a four-cornered sail is tethered only by one sheet and thus the sail is useless.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A line (rope) used to adjust the trim of a sail."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["A sail."],"tags":["nonstandard"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["The area of ice on which the game of curling is played."],"topics":["ball-games","curling","games","hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["A layer of veneer."],"tags":["nonstandard"]},{"glosses":["An extensive bed of an eruptive rock intruded between, or overlying, other strata."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"fore sheets; stern sheets","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The space in the forward or after part of a boat where there are no rowers."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["A distinct level or stage within a game."],"tags":["dated"],"topics":["video-games"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ʃiːt/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ʃit/","tags":["US"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"piece of paper","word":"page"},{"sense":"line","word":"rope"},{"sense":"expanse of material","word":"blanket"},{"sense":"expanse of material","word":"coat"},{"sense":"expanse of material","word":"coating"},{"sense":"expanse of material","word":"layer"}],"upstream_line":166448,"upstream_offset":776771175,"upstream_record_sha256":"95366b5fe269bcedfc16d4eea383f8c2ec8fbe3898cea4fb390e42ab08383a1d","word":"sheet"}
